<script type="text/javascript" nonce="KrzzU50mHO6nXEkZheglYtkI0M33OE2O">
	(function () {
		var userAgent = navigator.userAgent;
		var browser;

		if (userAgent.match(/firefox|fxios/i)) {
			browser = {
				url: "https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/addon/web-scraper/",
				name: "Firefox",
				icon: "firefox",
			};
		} else if (userAgent.match(/edg/i)) {
			browser = {
				url: "https://microsoftedge.microsoft.com/addons/detail/web-scraper-free-web-sc/jfmdjnnfminejadhegajcjbpgjofnkaj",
				name: "Edge",
				icon: "edge",
			};
		} else {
			browser = {
				url: "https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/web-scraper/jnhgnonknehpejjnehehllkliplmbmhn?hl=en",
				name: "Chrome",
				icon: "chrome",
			};
		}

		document.querySelectorAll(".install-extension").forEach(function (button) {
			button.setAttribute("href", browser.url);

			var icon = button.querySelector(".browser-icon");
			if (icon) {
				icon.className = icon.className.replace(/ws-icon-chrome-(500|dark)/g, "ws-icon-" + browser.icon + "-$1");
			}
		});

		document.querySelectorAll(".browser-name").forEach(function (el) {
			el.textContent = browser.name;
		});
	})();
</script>
